Consequently even the worst permitted measurement has
`m_b-delta>1/8-2*delta>200*delta`. The previous operational rate-resolution
gate therefore holds for both arms. The quotient's separate outward
arithmetic allowance remains required. The sample-error ceiling is an
acquisition requirement, not an observed accuracy or a quantity fitted from
the two-law discrepancy.

The shared [sample-rate observer](../../docs/contracts/RELATIONAL_DYNAMICS.md#relational-rate-samples)
implements the stencil and its exact error arithmetic without assuming
coefficient identification. The coefficient-sample helper delegates that
same calculation; phase samples do not acquire pure-form/consensus premises
merely by reusing it. Each rate interval feeds the existing contrast observer.

**What remains before evaluation.** Actual samples still need independent
error evidence. A numerical implementation must enclose the irrational
initial phase and non-binary form coordinates, evolution error, both phase
coordinate errors and any final subtraction error within `epsilon`.
The exact `1/3` and `pi/6` preparation cannot be treated as exactly binary64.
Uncertainty from different beta trajectories is model uncertainty, not
solver error. A validated interval-Taylor calculation is a possible bounded
software source; it must freeze source, parameter, order/grid and work budget
before response evaluation. Its candidate-specific solution enclosure would
verify that supplied simulator, not establish the unknown physical law.
Real measurements require independent preparation, gain, clock and noise
admission. The mathematical temporal budget is now feasible; no acquired
response, physical identification or connection-birth result follows.

**Retained static admission.** The local record
`artifacts/research/relational_capacity_discriminator/sampling-admission-v1.json`
retains the exact certificate, source inventory/digests and interpreter
version. Its sibling `sampling-admission-v1.source.zip` preserves 611 project
Python/configuration/control files. These files record a static admission,

The artifact directory is ignored by Git; a fresh clone can recompute the

### Bounded software verification of the phase-sampling protocol

<a id="capacity-discriminator-sampled-response"></a>

The [sampling instrument](../../benchmarks/relational_capacity_sampling_response.py)

**Fixed numerical method.** Use a single Taylor expansion from the initial
state, polynomial order six and whole-box remainder order seven. For each
coordinate, the shared flow-jet recurrence gives normalized coefficients
`c_j=z^(j)/j!`. Mathematical-pi and rational-form intervals enclose the exact
initial point. If `C7` encloses the seventh flow coefficient at every point
of the previously certified box, the integral remainder proves

\[
z_i(t)\in\sum_{j=0}^6 C_{ij}(z_0)t^j+C_{i7}(\mathcal B)t^7,
\qquad 0\le t\le1/32.

The prior first-exit proof keeps the actual solution inside `B`. This finite
Taylor theorem needs smoothness on that domain, not convergence of an
infinite power series. There is no stepping, recentering, adaptive order or

Then form the normalized contrast with the shared observer. Require all
